2026-10-30T07:35:16.5962423Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the round-6 PP4 bundle (MegaMoE split-ldsm and combine norm, four-heads-per-CTA Decode FA3 verify) with a fresh image

One image build and one PP4 serving run at concurrency 232 on top of the
9832.74 configuration (41005872470, main cd262ed). Every kernel that has
never served passes an in-job endurance gate before the workers start and
falls back to the last served one otherwise. This rule comes from the hang
of 40991744289. No profile pass is requested in this message.

The image must be rebuilt for three reasons:
- The DeepGEMM host series gained sm90-prefill-split-ldsm and
  sm90-prefill-combine-norm; 12 patches apply in order to a clean 1c8138a.
- kernels/fa3-verify gained decode-stream-h4.patch and the hardened
  prefill-pipe.patch; all 13 chain patches apply with fuzz 0 to the pinned
  sgl-attn archive.
- The SGLang overlay prefill-combine-norm was added; 30 overlays apply in
  order.

Changes and expected effect (power-cap factor in parentheses)

Prefill (PP0 period 115.3 / 115.0 / 118.7 ms at 9832.74)

1. MegaMoE four-stage-lean-split-ldsm (round 5 commits 4e41c52 and 5d816dd,
   brought in by f60eacb and ac84c1b).
   - Each warpgroup reads A once per K block with ldmatrix, issues both
     m64n128 halves in the RS form, and pre-rescales the next chunk's first
     half in FC1. The result is bit-exact.
   - Mainloop model ratios against split: FC1 0.877-0.880, FC2 0.902-0.904.
   - Expected -0.75..-1.25 ms per period (about 1).
   - Selected against lean-split. It has never served, so it must also pass
     600 calls per GPU on all twelve GPUs, or split serves.
   - Lean-split joined the served set after 41005872470.

2. Combine fused with the next layer's add-RMSNorm (49ea8e4,
   SGLANG_PREFILL_COMBINE_NORM).
   - The MegaMoE combine warp that owns a token row also adds the residual,
     takes the RMS, normalizes and writes the FP8 input and scales for the
     next layer.
   - This removes the separate norm kernel: 60.8 us per layer and 132 MB of
     y traffic.
   - Net expected 25-35 us per layer on 23/23/23/21 layers, -0.57..-0.80 ms
     per period (about 1).
   - The residual is bit-equal. Only the order of the square-sum changes;
     the CPU model gives 9.5e-7 byte mismatch and 8.2e-5 relative L2.
   - Gate select-firmus-prefill-megamoe.py --combine-norm: timed against the
     separate kernel, numeric limits on residual, q and scales, and 600
     consecutive calls with bit-identical repeats on every GPU, 90 s
     timeout.

3. The FA3 Prefill pipe row is hardened (80be935) but NOT screened in this
   run.
   - Fixed: the tile slot moved off live mbarrier storage, the producer
     publishes the KV block count, there is no barrier_O tail wait, and the
     row launches without PDL.
   - It is worth only -0.18..-0.30 ms per period. A repeat of the
     40991744289 hang would cost this whole measurement, so it waits for a
     later run.

Decode (step about 34 ms, ITL mean 15.40 ms)

4. Four-KV-heads-per-CTA stream-split FA3 verify row p32stream-h4-fp8
   (061019e).
   - It reads 512-byte token rows; the probe measured 3.25 TB/s against
     2.91 TB/s at 128 B.
   - Four consumer warpgroups, kBlockN 32, 3 stages, no main-loop spill.
   - The stream split cuts all request blocks into 132 equal ranges, so
     there is no wave tail. That tail is why the two-head rows lost at
     46/48.
   - Expected about -6 us per layer, -0.56 ms per step (-0.1..-1.0), about
     1.6% of the step (0.8-1.0).
   - Gates: the existing numeric gate, slot counts equal to the CPU split, at
     least 1 us/layer on every GPU at 44/46/48 in both blocks, and 300
     consecutive 94-layer graph replays per batch with a byte-identical
     final output. Otherwise the pin serves.

Totals: Prefill -1.1..-1.7%, Decode about -1.6%. The round-3 serving model
gives about 9,950-10,100 at c232.

Timeline:
- Prefill-GPU chain: MegaMoE selection, its endurance gate, then the
  combine-norm gate, about 3-3.5 min.
- Decode-GPU chain: about 4-4.5 min, unchanged within a few seconds.
- READY about 480-500 s. Slurm elapsed about 13:00-14:30.

Validation: full pytest suite exit 0, test_decode_step_overlays.py
separately, ruff clean, bash -n on the sbatch and provision.

Judging checklist:
- results/prefill-megamoe: the winner, the ldsm endurance result, and the
  combine-norm gate result.
- The decode-selection fa3.json h4 savings, slot check and endurance.
- score.json against 9832.74.
- PP0 period against 115.3 / 115.0 / 118.7 ms.
- ITL mean against 15.40 ms.
- Whether serving runs past warmup without a stall.
